# Items

2606 registered AP item names, including 2512 individual native items.

## Categorization

Weapons, armor, functional accessories, skill cards and usable battle/field supplies are Useful. Cosmetic outfits, treasure/materials, gifts, non-combat objects and accessories without a stat/effect benefit are Filler.

| Category | Named entries | Eligible when enabled | Default | YAML option |

| Melee weapons | 195 | 195 | On | randomize_melee_weapons |
| Ranged weapons / guns | 106 | 106 | On | randomize_guns |
| Armor | 200 | 200 | On | randomize_armor |
| Accessories | 439 | 439 | On | randomize_accessories |
| Consumables / infiltration tools | 552 | 551 | On | randomize_consumables |
| Skill cards | 459 | 459 | On | randomize_skill_cards |
| Materials / treasures | 168 | 168 | Off | randomize_materials |
| Key items | 120 | 0 | Off | Starting only |
| Outfits | 273 | 273 | Off | randomize_outfits |
